This design enables parallel inference of cell contents while conditioning each cell on global context encoded in the refined features. Experiments on two large datasets demonstrate consistent gains in cell localization and end-to-end recognition, while reducing overall inference time by around threefold.

</details>


### [69] [MoonSplat: Monocular Online Gaussian Splatting with Sim(3) Global Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17935)
*Guo Pu,Yixuan Han,Haofeng Li,Yao Zhang,Hui Zhou,Zhouhui Lian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MoonSplat，一种结合全局Sim(3)优化的在线体素化3DGS重建框架，通过颜色残差学习策略加速收敛，在相机位姿估计和渲染质量上达到SOTA，并实现了基于无人机的实际重建系统验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线3DGS方法存在两大挑战：缺乏全局优化导致相机位姿估计脆弱，以及在大规模或长序列场景中优化效率低下。

Method: 提出结合全局Sim(3)优化的体素化3DGS重建框架，实现相机跟踪和全局闭环优化；引入颜色残差学习策略加速体素化3DGS收敛。

Result: 在多样化的室内外数据集上，相机位姿估计精度和渲染质量均达到SOTA，同时保持实时效率；无人机实际部署验证了鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: MoonSplat有效解决了在线3DGS的位姿估计脆弱和优化效率低下的问题，为实际在线3D重建任务（如机器人、AR/VR、无人机）提供了稳健高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Online 3D reconstruction from monocular image sequences is a challenging and ongoing research topic. 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), leveraging its high-quality real-time rendering capability, empowers online 3D reconstruction to represent dense scenes with enhanced expressiveness, and thus holds great promise for a wide range of applications such as robotics and AR/VR. However, existing online 3DGS methods still suffer from some key challenges: fragile camera pose estimation due to the lack of global optimization, and low optimization efficiency in large-scale or long-sequence scenarios. To address these issues, we propose a robust and efficient online voxelized 3DGS reconstruction framework integrated with global $\text{Sim}(3)$ optimization, which enables reliable camera tracking and efficient global loop closure for both camera poses and voxelized 3DGS. To accelerate the convergence of the voxelized 3DGS, we further introduce a color residual learning strategy, which not only boosts optimization speed but also enhances rendering quality. Extensive experiments on diverse indoor and outdoor datasets demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in both camera pose estimation accuracy and rendering quality, while retaining real-time efficiency. Additionally, we develop and deploy a real-world UAV-based active reconstruction system grounded on our proposed method, validating its robustness and generalizability for practical online 3D reconstruction tasks. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/TrickyGo/MoonSplat.

</details>


### [70] [Plug-and-Adapt: Multimodal Coreference Resolution at First Sight with a Pretrained Alignment Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17950)
*Jinghan Wu,Jing Li,Ivor W. Tsang,Xuetao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种即插即用的多模态指代消解方法，通过预训练的对齐模型融合视觉与文本线索，无需目标数据集训练或依赖大模型，在CIN基准上取得SOTA性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCR方法依赖目标数据集的（部分）标注数据训练，缺乏直接可用性和泛化能力；而VLLM虽然提供零样本能力，但参数量巨大难以部署，且多数仅通过付费API访问。

Method: 首先使用视觉-语言对齐数据集预训练一个细粒度的文本与视觉上下文信息对齐模型；然后通过相似度聚合将对齐模型迁移至MCR任务，利用证据理论融合视觉和类别线索，提升有效性。

Result: 在CIN基准数据集上，CoNLL F1比专用SOTA方法提升5.31%，比流行VLLM提升2.12%；在带掩码的CIN和VCR-MCR数据集上分别验证了鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该方法无需在目标数据集上训练，不依赖资源密集型VLLM，即可实现即插即用的多模态指代消解，兼具有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Visual information helps resolve ambiguity in coreference resolution, leading to notable performance gains. However, existing Multi-modal Coreference Resolution (MCR) methods require training with (partially) annotated data from the target dataset before they can be applied, preventing their direct usability and raising concerns about generalization. While Vision-Language Large Models (VLLMs) with billions of parameters offer promising zero-shot capabilities, they remain largely inaccessible. Their massive size limits deployability, and many are only accessible through paid APIs. In this paper, we propose a plug-and-adapt method that strategically adapts a carefully pre-trained \emph{alignment model} for immediate use in MCR tasks, designed to eliminate the need for training on scarce benchmark datasets or relying on resource-intensive VLLMs. Specifically, we first pre-train a fine-grained alignment model between textual and visual contextual information using vision-language alignment datasets. We then repurpose the alignment model to MCR through similarity aggregation by fusing visual and categorical cues with evidence theory, thereby enhancing effectiveness. Experiments on the Coreference Image Narratives (CIN) benchmark dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, achieving a 5.31\% and 2.12\% improvement in CoNLL F1 over SOTA dedicated methods and popular VLLMs, respectively. We further evaluate our method on a masked CIN dataset for robustness testing and on a specially constructed VCR-MCR dataset for generalization assessment, with results confirming both capabilities.

</details>


### [71] [MLLMs Get It Right, Then Get It Wrong: Tracing and Correcting Late-Layer Textual Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17953)
*Xingming Li,Ao Cheng,Qiyao Sun,Xixiang He,Xuanyu Ji,Runke Huang,Qingyong Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文发现多模态大语言模型存在“晚层文本覆盖”现象：模型在中间层正确编码了视觉信息，但在最终输出层被文本信息覆盖。通过检测预测方向（失败向文本偏移85%，成功向视觉偏移89%）可识别这种覆盖，并提出无需训练的CALRD方法恢复被抑制的视觉预测，在五个模型上实现冲突基准最高9.4%的绝对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在视觉与文本冲突时总是偏向文本，即使图像提供了明确的相反证据。这种文本偏见在需要视觉定位的应用中构成风险，但其根本原因尚不清楚。本文旨在揭示这一现象的内在机制并开发相应的干预方法。

Method: 1. 通过分析模型中间层表示，发现“晚层文本覆盖”现象——视觉信息在中间层已正确编码，但在最终输出层被文本信息压制。2. 发现预测方向变化可作为正确性的信号：85%的失败预测向文本偏移，89%的成功预测向视觉偏移。3. 提出CALRD（Conflict-Aware Layer Reference Decoding）方法，通过检测被抑制的自信视觉预测并在推理时恢复它，无需训练或外部知识。

Result: 在五个不同架构的多模态大语言模型上，CALRD在冲突基准上实现了最高9.4%的绝对性能提升，同时基本保持标准性能不变。

Conclusion: 模型其实已经知道正确答案（视觉信息已在中间层编码），但在输出时未能保留。通过检测预测方向变化可以识别这种覆盖，而CALRD方法能有效恢复模型已经知道但未能保留的正确视觉预测。

Abstract: When vision contradicts text, multimodal large language models (MLLMs) consistently favor text, even when images provide clear evidence otherwise. This bias poses risks for applications requiring visual grounding, yet its cause remains unclear. In this paper, we uncover a surprising finding: models often get it right initially, forming correct vision-based predictions in their intermediate layers, before changing their minds and favoring text in the final output. We call this "late-layer textual override". The visual information is encoded, it simply does not survive to the output. More intriguingly, we find that how predictions change reveals whether they're correct: 85% of failures shift toward text, while 89% of successes shift toward vision. This directional signature enables a simple but powerful intervention: when we detect a confident visual prediction being suppressed, we restore it. We propose CALRD (Conflict-Aware Layer Reference Decoding), a training-free method that recovers overridden predictions at inference time. Experiments across five MLLMs of varying architectures demonstrate up to 9.4% absolute improvements on conflict benchmarks while largely preserving standard performance, without training or external knowledge. It recovers what the model already knew but failed to preserve.

</details>


### [72] [Beyond Visual Cues: CoT-Enhanced Reasoning for Semi-supervised Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17958)
*Yuming Chen,Yuxin Xie,Tao Zhou,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CERS框架，通过引入Chain-of-Thought推理和LLM生成的语言推理描述，解决半监督医学图像分割中视觉-语义不匹配问题，在边界模糊和语义不一致场景下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半监督医学图像分割方法过度依赖像素级视觉相似性，在视觉上相似但病理上不同的病灶（视觉-语义不匹配）场景中表现不佳，无法捕捉专家的诊断逻辑。

Method: 1) 构建由LLM生成的语言推理描述构成的知识池；2) 提出语义感知参考选择策略，先通过形态学筛选候选，再通过CoT一致性消除难负样本；3) 设计多尺度坐标注意力模块(MCAM)将推理上下文融入解码过程。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，CERS在解决边界模糊和语义不一致问题上优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 将CoT推理引入半监督医学图像分割能有效克服视觉-语义不匹配问题，提升分割的病理可解释性和准确性。

Abstract: Semi-supervised medical image segmentation has emerged as a dominant research problem in medical image analysis, mitigating annotation scarcity by leveraging consistency regularization on unlabeled data. However, existing approaches operate predominantly via visual pattern matching, relying heavily on pixel-level similarities. This visual-centric dependency often falters in clinical scenarios characterized by the visual-semantic mismatch, where visually similar lesions warrant distinct diagnostic conclusions, thus failing to capture the underlying diagnostic logic used by experts. To address this, we move beyond visual cues and propose CERS (CoT-Enhanced Reasoning Segmentation), a framework that integrates Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning to distinguish pathologically distinct cases. Specifically, we construct a knowledge pool enriched with linguistic reasoning descriptions generated by large language models (LLMs). A semantic-aware reference selection strategy is introduced to identify historical evidence, filtering candidates first by morphology, and then refining them via CoT consistency to eliminate hard negatives. Furthermore, a multi-scale coordinate attention module (MCAM) is designed to effectively fuse this reasoning-derived context into the decoding process. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of CERS against state-of-the-art approaches, particularly in resolving boundary ambiguities and semantic inconsistencies. The code is available at https://github.com/cymasuna/CERS.

</details>


### [73] [Robustness of Similarity-based Positional Encoding Under Rotations: Theoretical Analysis and Experimental Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17961)
*Andrea Santomauro,Luigi Portinale,Giorgio Leonardi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了相似性位置编码（simPE）在旋转变换下的鲁棒性，通过理论分析和实验验证表明：simPE并非旋转不变，但在Lipschitz假设下对旋转扰动是稳定的，并在四个数据集上比标准位置编码表现出更好的旋转鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管simPE在医学影像等对几何鲁棒性有需求的场景中展现出潜力，但其在几何扰动（尤其是旋转）下的理论行为尚未被充分刻画，因此需要系统研究其旋转鲁棒性。

Method: 结合形式化理论分析与实验验证：首先证明simPE通常不具备旋转不变性；然后在温和的Lipschitz假设下证明其对旋转扰动的稳定性，并推导出Frobenius范数下的显式扰动界；最后在四个数据集（Arrow、Shapes、Digits、FashionMNIST）上进行实验，固定训练/验证集朝向，对测试集施加递增旋转角度。

Result: 在所有数据集上，simPE在准确率、F1分数、精确率和召回率方面均一致优于标准可学习位置编码，尤其在中小角度旋转下优势明显，与理论稳定性保证一致。

Conclusion: simPE对旋转扰动具有理论保证的稳定性，在旋转鲁棒性方面优于标准位置编码，适合应用于可能发生轻微旋转的视觉任务。

Abstract: Positional encoding is a fundamental component of Transformer architectures, as it injects information about the spatial or sequential arrangement of inputs. Among recent alternatives to standard absolute and sinusoidal encodings, similarity-based positional encoding (simPE) has emerged as a flexible framework for representing positional structure through pairwise relations. simPE was originally designed for medical imaging applications, where geometric robustness is especially relevant: small rotations naturally arise during image acquisition, induced by imaging instruments, patient positioning, or slight acquisition misalignments. Despite its empirical promise, the theoretical behavior of simPE under geometric perturbations has not been fully characterized. In this paper, we study the robustness of simPE with respect to rotations, combining formal theoretical analysis with experimental validation. We first show that simPE is generally not rotation-invariant. We then prove that, under mild Lipschitz assumptions on the elementary components, simPE is stable under rotational perturbations and derive explicit perturbation bounds in Frobenius norm. We validate these findings experimentally on four controlled datasets--a synthetic Arrow dataset, a synthetic Shapes dataset (four geometric shape categories), a synthetic Digits dataset, and a benchmark image classification dataset (FashionMNIST)--in which training and validation images are kept in a fixed canonical orientation while test images are subjected to increasing rotation angles. Across all datasets, simPE consistently outperforms standard learned positional encoding in terms of accuracy, F1 score, precision, and recall under rotation, particularly in the small-to-moderate angle regime, corroborating the theoretical stability guarantees.

</details>


### [74] [Reload-Mamba: Hierarchical Anti-Dilution State-Space Modeling for Multi-Class Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17966)
*Sheng-Wei Chan,Hsin-Jui Pan,Jen-Shiun Chiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Reload-Mamba语义分割框架，针对Mamba状态空间模型在密集预测中因状态传播导致边界和细节响应衰减的问题，设计了三种分割专用机制：边界监督的局部细节先验、基于类别不确定性的Reload Gate（利用多类预测的像素级类别熵作为门控信号）、以及分层多级Reload机制。在ADE20K、Cityscapes和PASCAL VOC 2012上取得优异性能，消融实验验证了各设计的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mamba状态空间模型虽能实现线性时间的长程建模，但顺序状态传播会衰减多类语义分割中至关重要的边界敏感和细节敏感响应，现有抗稀释架构（针对二值化设计）无法直接适用于多类密集预测任务。

Method: (i) 边界监督的局部细节先验，使用真实边界掩码训练以识别需要响应恢复的区域；(ii) 类别不确定性感知的Reload Gate，利用预加载辅助头输出的像素级类别熵作为额外门控信号（该设计仅对多类密集预测有意义）；(iii) 分层多级Reload机制，在三个解码器层级应用抗稀释精炼并从顶至底融合恢复的表征。骨干为ConvNeXt-Tiny，配合多尺度解码器和四方向Mamba扫描及逐像素方向注意力。

Result: ADE20K上单尺度47.9% mIoU（多尺度48.9%），Cityscapes上83.2%单尺度mIoU，PASCAL VOC 2012 val上87.8% mIoU（ResNet-101+COCO预训练+DeepLab协议）。三个设计累积超越直接移植基线+2.2 mIoU。

Conclusion: Reload-Mamba通过三种分割专用设计有效缓解了Mamba状态传播导致的响应稀释问题，在多类语义分割任务上取得了显著性能提升，验证了针对任务特性定制Mamba架构的必要性和有效性。

Abstract: Mamba-based state space models offer linear-time long-range modeling for high-resolution dense prediction, but sequential state-space propagation can attenuate boundary-sensitive and detail-sensitive responses that are critical in multi-class semantic segmentation. We propose Reload-Mamba, a semantic segmentation framework that addresses this propagation-induced response dilution through three segmentation-specific designs: (i) a boundary-supervised local detail prior that is explicitly trained with ground-truth boundary masks to identify regions requiring response restoration; (ii) a class-uncertainty-aware Reload Gate that incorporates per-pixel class entropy from a pre-reload auxiliary head as an additional gating signal, a formulation that is informative only under multi-class dense prediction; and (iii) a hierarchical multi-level Reload mechanism that applies anti-dilution refinement at three decoder levels and fuses the restored representations top-down. Built upon a ConvNeXt-Tiny encoder with a multi-scale decoder and four-directional Mamba scanning with pixel-wise directional attention, Reload-Mamba achieves 47.9% single-scale (48.9% multi-scale) mIoU on ADE20K and 83.2% single-scale mIoU on Cityscapes. With ResNet-101 + COCO pre-training under the standard DeepLab-style protocol, Reload-Mamba reaches 87.8% mIoU on PASCAL VOC 2012 val. Controlled ablations show that each of the three segmentation-specific designs contributes beyond a direct port of the prior anti-dilution architecture proposed for binarization, cumulatively improving over the direct-port baseline by +2.2 mIoU on ADE20K.

</details>


### [75] [SegDINO: Introducing Multi-Scale Structure into DINO for Efficient Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17972)
*Sicheng Yang,Hongqiu Wang,Zhaohu Xing,Sixiang Chen,Qiuxia Yang,Yize Mao,Guang Yang,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SegDINO是一个高效的图像分割框架，通过将DINOv3骨干网络与轻量级尺度建模结合，利用Token Pyramid Adaptation (TPA)重组中间特征为伪多尺度层级，并采用Scale-Aware Decoding (SAD)进行尺度内精炼和自上而下的多尺度传播，在多个分割基准上达到SOTA效果且计算高效。同时提出了包含284名患者的胰腺肿瘤CT数据集PanCT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督DINO模型虽提供强迁移视觉表征，但直接应用于图像分割时，现有方法依赖复杂上采样的重型解码器，引入大量参数和计算开销。作者观察到在DINO特征中引入尺度比增加解码器容量更为关键。

Method: 提出SegDINO框架：(1) Token Pyramid Adaptation (TPA) - 将DINOv3中间特征重组为伪多尺度层级；(2) Scale-Aware Decoding (SAD) - 高效进行尺度内精炼和自上而下的多尺度传播；(3) 构建PanCT数据集 - 包含284名患者的专家标注胰腺肿瘤CT数据。

Result: 在PanCT和三个公开基准上，SegDINO达到了state-of-the-art的结果，同时保持高效性。

Conclusion: 验证了在DINO特征中引入尺度建模比增加解码器容量更有效，提出的轻量级尺度建模方法可高效实现高质量分割。

Abstract: Self-supervised DINO models provide strong transferable visual representations, yet applying them directly to image segmentation remains challenging. Existing approaches commonly rely on heavy decoders with complex upsampling, introducing substantial parameter and computational overhead. We observe that introducing scale into DINO features is far more critical than increasing decoder capacity. In this work, we present SegDINO, an efficient segmentation framework that integrates a DINOv3 backbone with lightweight scale modeling. SegDINO introduces Token Pyramid Adaptation (TPA) to reorganize intermediate DINO features into a pseudo multi-scale hierarchy, and Scale-Aware Decoding (SAD) for efficient intra-scale refinement and top-down multi-scale propagation. We further curate PanCT, a new CT dataset containing 284 patients with expert-annotated pancreatic tumors, to assess SegDINO's ability to handle difficult small-lesion cases. Extensive experiments on PanCT and three public benchmarks demonstrate that SegDINO achieves state-of-the-art results with high efficiency. The code is available at https://github.com/script-Yang/segdino_v2.

</details>


### [76] [Gaussian Light Field Splatting: A Physical Prior-Driven Vision Transformer for Unsupervised Low-Light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17985)
*Yuhan Chen,Wenxuan Yu,Guofa Li,Fuchen Li,Kunyang Huang,Yicui Shi,Ying Fang,Wenbo Chu,Keqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GLFS提出一种基于高斯光场溅射的视觉Transformer，将连续物理光照建模引入Transformer架构，通过各向异性高斯基函数叠加表示场景光照，并在自注意力中引入物理引导偏置自适应推断空间增益场，配合颜色向量角度损失和亮度边缘损失，实现复杂非均匀光照下的低光图像增强，达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督低光图像增强方法在复杂非均匀光照下常出现局部曝光失衡和颜色失真，且大多数视觉Transformer缺乏对光照退化物理先验的显式建模机制。

Method: 1) 场景光照由各向异性高斯基函数叠加表示；2) 在自注意力中引入物理引导偏置，自适应推断空间增益场；3) 提出颜色向量角度损失和亮度边缘损失，分别增强色调一致性和局部细节结构保真度。

Result: 大量消融实验和定量评估表明，GLFS在光照校正和细节保持方面具有明显优势，取得了SOTA性能。

Conclusion: GLFS为低光图像增强提供了一种新的表示范式，有效解决了局部曝光失衡和颜色失真问题。

Abstract: Existing unsupervised low-light image enhancement methods often encounter local exposure imbalance and color distortion under complex non-uniform illumination. In addition, most Vision Transformers lack an explicit mechanism for modeling the physical priors of illumination degradation. To address these limitations, we propose GLFS, a Gaussian light field splatting-based Vision Transformer that integrates continuous physical illumination modeling from Gaussian splatting into the Transformer architecture. In GLFS, scene illumination is represented by a superposition of anisotropic Gaussian basis functions. Physics-guided biases are introduced into self-attention to adaptively infer a spatial gain field, enabling accurate and uniform restoration under complex illumination. To reduce color bias and structural degradation during enhancement, a color-vector angular loss and a luminance-edge loss are further developed. These losses enforce hue consistency and improve the structural fidelity of local details. Extensive ablation studies and quantitative evaluations show that GLFS provides clear advantages in illumination correction and detail preservation. It achieves state-of-the-art performance and offers a new representation paradigm for low-light image enhancement.

</details>


### [77] [Recover Semantics First, Generate Better: Improved Latent Modeling for 3D MRI Reconstruction and Cross-Contrast Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17989)
*Yonghao Chen,Sicheng Yang,Rui Tang,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种语义优先的潜在建模框架（RSF），用于3D MRI重建和跨对比度合成。该方法通过潜在协调编码器（LHE）捕获全局解剖依赖性，通过语义恢复块（SRB）注入自监督语义先验以增强潜在空间的对比度感知可分性，并引入解剖感知频率损失（AFL）自适应保留诊断相关的高频结构。在两个公共多对比度MRI数据集上，该方法在重建保真度和跨对比度合成质量上均取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D MRI潜在压缩架构存在三个关键问题：未充分保留长程解剖连贯性、丢弃临床有意义的语义信息、依赖导致过度平滑重建的优化目标，这些缺陷最终损害了后续生成模型的性能。因此需要一种更好的潜在建模框架来提升3D MRI重建与跨对比度合成质量。

Method: 1) 提出潜在协调编码器（LHE），用于捕获全局解剖依赖性，确保体积表示的连贯性；2) 设计语义恢复块（SRB），从自监督语义教师中注入高层先验，增强潜在空间中对比度感知的可分性；3) 提出解剖感知频率损失（AFL），自适应保留诊断相关的高频结构。

Result: 在两个公共多对比度MRI数据集上，该方法在重建保真度和跨对比度合成质量方面均取得一致且显著的改进。

Conclusion: 所提出的语义优先潜在建模框架有效解决了现有3D MRI压缩架构的局限性，提升了重建与跨对比度合成性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-contrast magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) provides complementary information for clinical diagnosis. However, acquiring all MRI sequences is often time-consuming and costly. Recent generative models perform cross-contrast synthesis to address this issue by inferring absent contrasts from the available ones. Nevertheless, synthesizing 3D MRI presents significant challenges. Due to the massive volume sizes, operating directly in the pixel space is computationally prohibitive; therefore, a common approach is to first compress the 3D volumes into a latent space and subsequently train generative models in that space. We observe that existing compression architectures face several critical issues: they under-preserve long-range anatomical coherence, discard clinically meaningful semantics, and rely on optimization objectives that lead to over-smoothed reconstructions. Ultimately, these shortcomings compromise the performance of subsequent generative models. In this work, we propose a semantics-first latent modeling framework for 3D MRI reconstruction and cross-contrast synthesis. Specifically, we introduce a Latent Harmonization Encoder (LHE) to capture global anatomical dependencies, ensuring coherent volumetric representations. To mitigate semantic degradation during latent compression, we further design a Semantic Recovery Block (SRB) that injects high-level priors from a self-supervised semantic teacher, enhancing contrast-aware separability in the latent space. Additionally, we propose an Anatomy-aware Frequency Loss (AFL) to adaptively preserve diagnostically relevant high-frequency structures. Extensive experiments on two public multi-contrast MRI datasets demonstrate consistent improvements in reconstruction fidelity and cross-contrast synthesis quality. Our code is available at https://github.com/script-Yang/RSF.

</details>


### [78] [AIGS-Net: Compact Illumination Field Modeling via 2D Gaussian Splatting for Fast Low-Light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17998)
*Yuhan Chen,Kunyang Huang,Fuchen Li,Zhuohan Qin,Guofa Li,Wenbo Chu,Keqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AIGS-Net，一种仅需约40个可学习参数的极轻量级低光图像增强网络，通过自适应2D高斯溅射照明场建模、零参数多尺度上下文编码和噪声抑制策略，在LOL和LSRW基准上实现了增强质量与推理效率的有效平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低光图像增强方法在照明场建模的表征能力与计算复杂度之间存在瓶颈，亟需一种兼顾质量与效率的轻量化方案。

Method: 提出自适应照明高斯溅射网络（AIGS-Net），核心包括：(1) 输入自适应的2D高斯溅射照明场，通过相对亮度统计动态调制高斯基函数的不透明度，并采用有序alpha合成渲染空间变化的照明补偿；(2) 零参数非线性多尺度上下文编码模块，无需额外卷积权重提取低频结构与局部对比度线索；(3) 噪声掩码估计、锁定单通道Gamma映射、跨通道一致性正则化和目标颜色对齐约束，以抑制噪声放大和传感器导致的颜色偏差。

Result: 在LOL和LSRW基准上，AIGS-Net在仅需约40个可学习参数的情况下，提升了细节恢复和颜色保真度，实现了增强质量与极端推理效率的有效权衡。

Conclusion: AIGS-Net通过极少量参数实现了高质量的低光图像增强，为资源受限场景下的实用化部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Existing low-light image enhancement methods often face a bottleneck between the representation capacity of illumination-field modeling and computational complexity. To address this issue, this paper proposes an Adaptive Illumination Gaussian Splatting Network (AIGS-Net), an ultra-lightweight architecture for fast low-light enhancement. Unlike conventional static priors, AIGS-Net constructs an input-adaptive 2D Gaussian Splatting illumination field. The opacity of Gaussian basis functions is dynamically modulated by relative luminance statistics of the input image, and spatially varying illumination compensation is rendered through ordered alpha compositing. To guide adaptive illumination compensation efficiently, a zero-parameter nonlinear multiscale contextual encoding module is introduced to extract low-frequency structures and local contrast cues without additional convolutional weights. To suppress noise amplification and sensor-induced color bias, AIGS-Net integrates noise-mask estimation, locked single-channel Gamma mapping, cross-channel consistency regularization, and target color-alignment constraints. Experiments on LOL and LSRW benchmarks show that AIGS-Net improves detail recovery and color fidelity while requiring only approximately 40 learnable parameters, achieving an effective trade-off between enhancement quality and extreme inference efficiency.

</details>


### [79] [PhaseWin: An Efficient Search Algorithm for Faithful Visual Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18008)
*Zihan Gu,Ruoyu Chen,Junchi Zhang,Li Liu,Xiaochun Cao,Hua Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhaseWin是一种高效的可信视觉归因算法，通过分阶段窗口搜索将计算复杂度从O(n²)降至O(n)，在保持接近贪心搜索可信度的同时大幅减少模型前向传播次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉归因方法中，穷举搜索代价指数级，贪心搜索也需要二次方数量的模型评估，计算效率低下，亟需更高效的算法。

Method: PhaseWin将贪心区域选择重组为分阶段窗口搜索过程，交替进行全局候选筛选、自适应剪枝和局部窗口细化，避免每一步都重新评估全部候选区域。

Result: 在图像分类、目标检测、视觉定位和图像描述等任务上，PhaseWin以最少的前向传播次数达到了最高的可信度，经验证实现了从O(n²)到O(n)的复杂度降低。

Conclusion: PhaseWin在保证接近贪心搜索可信度的前提下，实现了可控的线性计算复杂度，为高效视觉归因提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Visual attribution is a fundamental tool for interpreting modern vision and vision-language models, particularly when their decisions must be inspected, diagnosed, or audited. Its goal is to explain how a model's decision depends on local regions of the visual input, typically by assigning an importance ordering over candidate image regions. Given an image partitioned into $n$ regions, faithful attribution can be cast as an ordered subset-search problem, in which progressively inserting the selected regions should recover the target model response as early as possible. Exhaustive search over region subsets incurs exponential cost, while the widely used greedy search still requires a quadratic number of model evaluations, because every selection step rescores all remaining candidates. We propose PhaseWin, an efficient subset-search algorithm for faithful visual attribution. PhaseWin reorganizes greedy region selection into a phased window-search procedure: rather than re-evaluating the full candidate set at every step, it alternates between global candidate screening, adaptive pruning, and localized window refinement, while preserving the essential region-ranking behavior of greedy search. We analyze PhaseWin under monotone evidence-accumulation conditions and show that, under feature-level structural assumptions, it attains controllable linear evaluation complexity together with near-greedy faithfulness guarantees. Extensive experiments on image classification, object detection, visual grounding, and image captioning show that, among all compared attribution methods, PhaseWin reaches high faithfulness with the fewest forward passes, empirically realizing the predicted reduction from $O(n^2)$ to $O(n)$. The code is available at https://github.com/Qihuai27/phasewin-va.

</details>


### [80] [When LLMs Analyze Scars: From Images to Clinically-Meaningful Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18063)
*Ruman Wang,Hangting Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ScaFE框架，利用大语言模型生成特征提取代码，将高维医学图像转化为低维可解释特征，在数据稀缺的瘢痕分类任务中优于端到端深度学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分类面临数据稀缺困境（标注成本高、隐私限制、疾病罕见），尤其在病理性瘢痕分类中，区分瘢痕疙瘩和增生性瘢痕需要专业知识且标注图像极少。

Method: ScaFE框架：利用LLM根据临床评分标准（如Vancouver Scar Scale）生成确定性Python代码，将高维图像转化为低维、临床可解释的特征表示，而非使用LLM作为端到端分类器。

Result: 在瘢痕分类任务中，ScaFE在数据有限条件下持续优于端到端深度学习基线和使用LLM作为黑盒分类器的方法。

Conclusion: 将LLM作为知识驱动的特征工程师是一种有前景的方向，可实现数据高效、隐私保护且临床可解释的医学AI系统。

Abstract: Medical image classification faces a fundamental dilemma: while deep learning models achieve remarkable performance at scale, real-world clinical scenarios often suffer from severe data scarcity due to annotation costs, privacy constraints, and disease rarity. This challenge is particularly pronounced in pathological scar classification, where differentiating keloids from hypertrophic scars requires subtle expert knowledge and labeled images are extremely limited. We propose a novel paradigm that repositions large language models (LLMs) as knowledge-driven feature engineers rather than end-to-end classifiers. We call this framework ScaFE (Scar Feature Engineering). Our key insight is that LLMs encode rich medical knowledge that can be externalized as executable feature extraction code, enabling the transformation of high-dimensional images into low-dimensional, clinically interpretable representations. Specifically, we prompt an LLM with established scar assessment criteria to generate deterministic Python code that extracts features aligned with clinical scoring systems such as the Vancouver Scar Scale. Our approach offers three key advantages: (1) data efficiency, achieving robust performance with limited training samples by decoupling knowledge acquisition from statistical learning; (2) privacy preservation, as raw images are processed locally without exposure to external LLMs; and (3) interpretability, through explicit features grounded in clinical reasoning. Extensive experiments on scar classification demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms end-to-end deep learning baselines or using LLMs as black-box classifiers under limited data conditions, establishing a promising direction for integrating LLMs into data-efficient and clinically transparent medical AI systems.

</details>


### [81] [HLS-GPT: A Generative Pretrained Transformer (GPT) for Continental-Scale NASA Harmonized Landsat and Sentinel-2 (HLS) Reflectance Reconstruction Across All Bands on Arbitrary Dates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18115)
*Junjie Li,Hankui K. Zhang,David P. Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HLS-GPT，一个大规模生成式预训练Transformer模型，用于重建NASA Harmonized Landsat Sentinel-2 30米地表反射率数据，覆盖所有波段、任意日期和任意像素位置。模型采用分层Transformer架构处理Landsat和Sentinel-2的不同光谱波段配置，基于单像素12个月时间序列操作。使用美国本土超过25万训练像素的9年HLS时间序列训练，采用随机裁剪和掩码策略，重建性能优于传统方法和NASA-IBM Prithvi模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法在Landsat和Sentinel-2反射率时间序列重建方面存在光谱覆盖受限、地理可扩展性有限、或基于patch设计且时间上下文短等局限性，亟需一个能覆盖所有波段、任意日期和任意像素位置的大规模重建模型。

Method: 采用分层Transformer架构（HLS-GPT），基于单像素12个月时间序列，使用随机裁剪和掩码策略，掩码50%的有效观测，训练模型从剩余观测中重建被掩码的反射率值。训练数据涵盖美国本土超过25万像素的9年HLS时间序列。

Result: 在超过6.2万独立测试像素上，逐次留一观测评估中所有HLS光谱波段重建RMSE低于0.026，可见光波段相对RMSE低于35%，其他波段低于13%。缺失10%-50%观测时全波段RMSE低于0.028。在9个独立109×109 km CONUS HLS图块上优于传统方法和NASA-IBM Prithvi模型。

Conclusion: HLS-GPT在不同地表条件下（包括复杂作物物候和稀疏不规则观测）均表现出鲁棒的重建能力，且能处理Landsat缺少红边波段的问题，红边波段误差与红波段和近红外波段相当。

Abstract: Recent deep learning methods for Landsat and Sentinel-2 reflectance time series reconstruction remain limited by restricted spectral coverage, limited geographic scalability, or patch-based designs with short temporal contexts. We present HLS-GPT, a large-scale generative pretrained Transformer model for reconstructing NASA Harmonized Landsat Sentinel-2 30 m surface reflectance for all bands, any date, and any pixel location. HLS-GPT uses a hierarchical Transformer architecture to handle the different spectral band configurations of Landsat and Sentinel-2 and operates on single-pixel 12-month time series. To capture geographic and seasonal variability, the model was trained with nine years of HLS time series from more than 0.25 million training pixels across the conterminous United States. A random cropping and masking strategy extracts 12-month periods with varying start dates across epochs, masks 50% of valid observations, and trains the model to reconstruct the masked reflectance values from the remaining observations. Evaluation using more than 62,000 independent test pixels shows robust reconstruction under diverse land surface conditions, including complex crop phenology and sparse, irregular observations. Leave-one-observation-out evaluation achieved reconstruction RMSE below 0.026 for all HLS spectral bands, with relative RMSE below 35% for visible bands and below 13% for other bands. Red-edge band errors were comparable to red and near-infrared errors despite the absence of red-edge bands on Landsat. Sensitivity analyses that randomly masked 10% to 90% of test observations showed only modest degradation when 10% to 50% of observations were masked, with all-band RMSE below 0.028. Image reconstruction over nine independent 109 by 109 km CONUS HLS tiles further demonstrates that HLS-GPT outperforms two conventional methods and the NASA-IBM Prithvi model.

</details>


### [82] [Predicting Immune Biomarkers with MultiModal Mixture-of-Expert Pathology Foundation Models Empowers Precision Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18123)
*Tianyu Liu,Ziqing Wang,Zhaokang Liang,Tong Ding,Peter Humphrey,Lorraine Colón-Cartagena,Emily Ling-Lin Pai,Kenneth Tou En Chang,Mohamed Kahila,Jonathan Chong Kai Liew,Tinglin Huang,Rex Ying,Kaize Ding,Faisal Mahmood,Wengong Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MixTIME是一个多模态基础模型，采用混合专家（MoE）架构整合三种病理基础模型（UNIv2、CONCHv1.5、STPath），从HE全切片图像中预测多重免疫荧光（mIF）蛋白表达，在17个蛋白标记物上达到SOTA性能，并显著提升空间域识别、生存预测和病理报告生成等下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测免疫生物标志物的方法局限于单一图像模态，分辨率不足且未能充分利用互补的临床和生物信息，难以满足精准肿瘤学对多模态整合分析的需求。

Method: MixTIME采用混合专家（MoE）架构，集成三种不同模态的病理基础模型（纯图像UNIv2、图像文本CONCHv1.5、图像转录组STPath），通过可学习路由器动态加权专家贡献，并采用分布与趋势感知的损失函数进行训练，实现像素级和切片级的mIF蛋白表达预测。

Result: 在两个不同规模的数据集上，MixTIME在17个蛋白标记物的相关性指标上达到SOTA性能；预测的mIF谱显著提升了空间域识别、生存预测和AI辅助病理报告生成等下游任务，并经多机构病理专家验证；同时实现了临床时间点蛋白表达动态的纵向追踪，揭示了与耐药和免疫抑制相关的蛋白基因互作模式。

Conclusion: MixTIME为计算病理学中的多模态生物标志物发现和临床转化提供了一个可扩展的框架。

Abstract: Predicting immune biomarkers associated with the tumor immune microenvironment (TIME) is critical for advancing precision oncology, yet existing approaches are largely limited to single image modalities and suffer from insufficient resolution and incomplete utilization of complementary clinical and biological information. Here we introduce MixTIME, a multimodal foundation model that leverages a mixture-of-experts (MoE) architecture to integrate pathology foundation models trained across distinct modalities: image only (UNIv2), image text (CONCHv1.5), and image transcriptomic (STPath) representations for pixel-level and slide-level prediction of multiplex immunofluorescence (mIF) protein expression from hematoxylin and eosin (HE) whole-slide images. MixTIME employs a learnable router to dynamically weight expert contributions and is trained with a distribution- and tendency-aware loss function. Benchmarked on two datasets of different scales, MixTIME achieves state-of-the-art performance across 17 protein markers as measured by correlation metrics. The predicted mIF profiles substantially enhance downstream tasks, including spatial domain identification, survival prediction, and AI-assisted pathology report generation validated by expert pathologists from multiple institutes across the world. Furthermore, MixTIME enables longitudinal tracking of protein expression dynamics across clinical time points and reveals protein gene interaction patterns linked to drug resistance and immune suppression in tumor microenvironments. Collectively, MixTIME provides a scalable framework for multimodal biomarker discovery and clinical translation in computational pathology.

</details>


### [83] [Neural Tree Reconstruction for the Open Forest Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18153)
*Marissa Ramirez de Chanlatte,Arjun Rewari,Trevor Darrell,Derek J. N. Young*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 开放森林观测站（OFO）是一个跨大学合作项目，旨在为生态学家、土地管理者和公众提供低成本森林测绘。目前OFO使用经典运动恢复结构（SfM）技术创建3D树图，但该方法存在伪影、细节不足、林下可见性差等问题。本文探讨将神经辐射场（NeRF）引入OFO数据集，以提升3D重建质量，并说明高质量3D重建对林业应用的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OFO现有基于经典SfM技术的3D森林地图存在伪影、细节缺失、林下区域重建困难等缺陷，这些误差可能传播到下游科学任务（如野火模拟），因此需要更先进的3D重建方法来提升数据质量。

Method: 探讨将NeRF方法融入OFO数据集，规划未来支持更先进3D视觉模型的工作，并通过具体林业应用场景说明高质量重建的重要性。

Result: NeRF相比经典SfM能产生更高质量的3D重建结果，对稀疏视图更鲁棒，且支持数据驱动先验。

Conclusion: 将NeRF等先进3D重建技术纳入OFO可以提升森林地图质量，减少下游林业应用中的误差传播，对气候科学中的重新造林、野火减灾和碳汇监测具有重要意义。

Abstract: The Open Forest Observatory (OFO) is a collaboration across universities and other partners to make low-cost forest mapping accessible to ecologists, land managers, and the general public. The OFO is building both a database of geospatial forest data as well as open-source methods and tools for forest mapping by uncrewed aerial vehicle. Such data are useful for a variety of climate applications including prioritizing reforestation efforts, informing wildfire hazard reduction, and monitoring carbon sequestration. In the current iteration of the OFO's forest map database, 3D tree maps are created using classical structure-from-motion techniques. This approach is prone to artifacts, lacks detail, and has particular difficulty on the forest floor where the input data (overhead imagery) has limited visibility. These reconstruction errors can potentially propagate to the downstream scientific tasks (e.g. a wildfire simulation.) Advances in 3D reconstruction, including methods like Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF), produce higher quality results that are more robust to sparse views and support data-driven priors. We explore ways to incorporate NeRFs into the OFO dataset, outline future work to support even more state-of-the-art 3D vision models, and describe the importance of high-quality 3D reconstructions for forestry applications.

</details>


### [84] [ReAge3D: Re-Aging 3D Faces with View Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18156)
*Libing Zeng,Li Ma,Mingming He,Ning Yu,Paul Debevec,Nima Khademi Kalantari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于扩散模型的3D人脸变老/变年轻框架，通过中心向外编辑传播策略和Masked-DiffReaging过程，实现多视图一致的精细年龄编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D编辑方法适用于粗粒度语义变化，但在面部变老任务中，不同视角间即使微小不一致也会导致年龄相关细节过度平滑，缺乏保真度。

Method: 1) 训练2D扩散变老模型DiffReaging（基于合成图像对）；2) 提出中心向外编辑传播策略：先编辑正面视图，再通过warping和Masked-DiffReaging重建其余视图，在扩散每一步注入已有内容确保一致性；3) 利用多视图一致的重建图像优化3D表示。

Result: 在视觉和定量指标上均优于现有3D编辑方法，实现了平滑、精细的年龄变换控制。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了3D人脸变老中的多视图一致性和细节保持问题，支持可控的精细年龄编辑。

Abstract: We present a novel framework for realistic and controllable 3D face re-aging which produces highly detailed, identity-preserving results. Existing 3D editing methods, while effective for coarse semantic changes, are not well suited for re-aging, as even small inconsistencies across re-aged 2D views can lead to over-smoothing of subtle but perceptually important age-related details. To address this challenge, we first introduce a 2D diffusion-based re-aging model, DiffReaging, trained on synthetically generated image pairs. We further propose a center-out editing propagation strategy that leverages this re-aging model to reconstruct multi-view-consistent re-aged images. Specifically, starting from a re-aged frontal pivot view, we reconstruct the remaining views through warping and our proposed Masked-DiffReaging process. By injecting existing content at every step of the diffusion process, Masked-DiffReaging ensures that the reconstructed regions remain coherent with existing pixels. The resulting consistent set of re-aged views supervises the optimization of the re-aged 3D representation. Our method outperforms existing 3D editing techniques both visually and quantitatively, enabling smooth, fine-grained control over age transformations in 3D face models.

</details>


### [85] [EgoCS-400K: An Egocentric Gameplay Dataset for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18180)
*Rongjin Guo,Dong Liang,Yuhao Liu,Fang Liu,Tianyu Huang,Gerhard P. Hancke,Rynson W. H. Lau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了EgoCS-400K，一个基于《反恐精英》职业比赛Demo的大规模第一人称数据集，包含超过40万段第一人称视频和1万小时游戏时长，提供时间对齐的动作-视频-语言轨迹，用于支撑交互式世界模型的训练与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频数据缺乏可执行动作和状态标注，机器人数据场景多样性有限且成本高昂，模拟器缺乏大规模人类交互轨迹，亟需一种能桥接被动视频、可控仿真与真实世界具身数据的大规模交互数据集。

Method: 从CS/CS2职业比赛Demo中提取玩家状态、视角方向、移动/按键输入、视角变化、武器使用、游戏事件及回合上下文，渲染干净的第一人称视频，构建包含40万+视频、1万+小时、1000+场比赛、4万+回合、13张地图、每回合10个视角的大规模数据集。

Result: EgoCS-400K支撑多项交互视觉建模任务：动作条件未来预测、状态/事件感知场景展开、回放基础字幕生成、智能体自我中心动作理解。

Conclusion: EgoCS-400K作为被动网络视频、可控游戏仿真与高成本真实世界具身数据之间的实用桥梁，为交互式世界模型提供了大规模、多模态、时间对齐的训练数据基础。

Abstract: The shift from video generation to interactive world modeling places new demands on data: beyond captioned videos, world models require temporally aligned video-action-language trajectories grounded in the actions, camera motion, states, and events that drive future scene changes. However, such data is difficult to obtain at scale. Web video datasets offer broad visual coverage but lack executable actions and reliable states; robotic datasets provide action and state supervision but are costly and limited in scene diversity; and existing simulators often lack large-scale human-driven interaction trajectories. In this paper, we introduce EgoCS-400K, a large-scale replay-grounded egocentric Counter-Strike dataset for world models, built from public professional CS and CS2 match demos that preserve human gameplay trajectories and enable parsing, replaying, rendering, and temporal alignment. We extract player states, view directions, movements, keyboard/button inputs, view-angle changes, weapon usage, game events, and round-level context, and render clean first-person videos from the same trajectories. EgoCS-400K contains over 400,000 first-person videos and 10,000 hours of gameplay from more than 1,000 matches and 40,000 rounds, covering 13 maps and 10 player viewpoints per round. It supports a range of interactive visual modeling tasks, including action-conditioned future prediction, state- and event-aware scene rollout, replay-grounded captioning, and agent egocentric action understanding. By connecting visual observations with human actions, camera motion, game states, and events at scale, EgoCS-400K serves as a practical bridge between passive web videos, controllable game simulation, and costly real-world embodied data.

</details>


### [86] [Adaptive Volumetric Mechanical Property Fields Invariant to Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18231)
*Rishit Dagli,Donglai Xiang,Vismay Modi,Xuning Yang,Gavriel State,David I. W. Levin,Maria Shugrina*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdaVoMP提出了一种基于稀疏自适应体素结构（SAV）和稀疏Transformer编码器-解码器的方法，用于预测3D物体的高分辨率空间变化材料属性（杨氏模量、泊松比、密度），分辨率比先前最优方法提升16³倍，且计算效率更高，能够将复杂3D物体转化为可直接用于物理仿真的资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数3D资产缺乏准确的力学属性信息（杨氏模量、泊松比、密度），而这些属性对于数字世界的可靠物理仿真至关重要。

Method: 提出SAV（稀疏自适应体素）结构高效表示输入形状和材料场；用稀疏Transformer编码器-解码器模型替代VoMP的固定体素模型，以自回归方式为每个输入形状生成唯一的SAV来表示其材料属性。

Result: 分辨率比先前最优方法提升16³倍，在更少的测试时计算量下估计出更准确的体积属性，能够将高分辨率复杂3D物体转化为可直接用于物理仿真的资产，实现逼真的可变形仿真。

Conclusion: AdaVoMP在材料属性预测的分辨率、准确性和内存效率方面均超越了现有方法，为任意3D物体生成仿真就绪的资产提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate mechanical properties (or materials) Young's modulus ($E$), Poisson's ratio ($ν$) and density ($ρ$) are essential for reliable physics simulation of digital worlds, but most 3D assets lack this information. We propose AdaVoMP, a method for predicting accurate dense spatially-varying ($E$, $ν$, $ρ$) for input 3D objects across representations, improving the resolution, accuracy, and memory efficiency over the state-of-the-art. The foundation of our technique is a sparse and adaptive voxel structure SAV that efficiently represents both the input 3D shape and the material field output. We replace the fixed-voxel model of the most accurate prior method, VoMP, with a novel sparse transformer encoder-decoder model that learns to generate a unique SAV autoregressively for every input shape to represent its materials, achieving a resolution $16^3\times$ higher than prior art. Experiments show that AdaVoMP estimates more accurate volumetric properties, even with lesser test-time compute than all prior art. This allows us to convert high-resolution complex 3D objects into simulation-ready assets, resulting in realistic deformable simulations.

</details>


### [87] [EventDrive: Event Cameras for Vision-Language Driving Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18242)
*Dongyue Lu,Rong Li,Ao Liang,Lingdong Kong,Wei Yin,Lai Xing Ng,Benoit R. Cottereau,Camille Simon Chane,Wei Tsang Ooi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EventDrive是首个将事件相机与RGB帧统一的大规模基准和模型套件，覆盖自动驾驶的感知、理解、预测和规划四大核心维度，通过多视界事件金字塔和时序混合专家模块，显著提升了时间精度、运动感知和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件感知视觉-语言模型仅限于通用感知任务，未能揭示事件传感如何在整个驾驶循环中贡献推理和决策，缺乏统一基准来系统评估事件流在自动驾驶各环节的作用。

Method: (1) 构建EventDrive大规模基准，统一事件流、RGB帧和语言监督，覆盖感知、理解、预测、规划四大维度；(2) 提出EventDrive-VLM，包含多视界事件金字塔（Multi-horizon Event Pyramid）和时序-视界混合专家模块（Temporal-horizon Mixture-of-Experts），自适应编码和融合异步事件与帧基信息。

Result: 在多种任务上的全面评估表明，事件流在时间精度、运动感知和鲁棒性方面带来了显著提升，将事件传感置于驾驶智能的核心位置。

Conclusion: 事件流是帧基感知的有力补充，尤其在模糊、眩光和快速运动等挑战场景下，EventDrive框架验证了事件传感对自动驾驶推理与决策的实质性贡献。

Abstract: Event cameras sense the world through asynchronous brightness changes with microsecond latency and high dynamic range, offering motion fidelity far beyond frame-based sensors and capturing temporal structure that conventional exposures often miss. These properties make events a powerful complement to RGB in autonomous driving, especially under blur, glare, and rapid motion, where frame-based perception can become unreliable. However, existing event-aware vision-language models remain limited to generic perception and do not reveal how event sensing contributes to reasoning and decision-making across the full driving loop. We present EventDrive, a large-scale benchmark and model suite that unifies event streams, RGB frames, and language supervision across four core dimensions: Perception, Understanding, Prediction, and Planning, covering captions, structured QA, grounding, motion-state recognition, trajectory forecasting, and planning tasks. Building on this foundation, EventDrive-VLM introduces a multi-horizon event pyramid and a temporal-horizon mixture-of-experts module to adaptively encode and fuse asynchronous and frame-based information for downstream reasoning. Comprehensive evaluation across diverse tasks shows that event streams provide substantial gains in temporal precision, motion awareness, and robustness, bringing event sensing into the center of driving intelligence.

</details>


### [88] [Unified Multimodal Autoregressive Modeling with Shared Context-Visual Tokenizer is Key to Unification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18249)
*Wujian Peng,Lingchen Meng,Yuxuan Cai,Xianwei Zhuang,Yuhuan Yang,Rongyao Fang,Chenfei Wu,Junyang Lin,Zuxuan Wu,Shuai Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniAR提出统一自回归框架，使用单一离散视觉分词器桥接视觉理解与生成，通过多级特征融合、无查找量化、并行比特级预测和扩散解码器，在图像生成和编辑上达到SOTA，同时保持理解任务竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态建模方法依赖两个不同的视觉分词器，导致表示空间分裂，阻碍了真正的统一建模。作者希望用一个统一的离散视觉分词器来桥接理解与生成，使模型能直接解读自身生成的视觉token而无需额外重编码。

Method: 1) 单一离散视觉分词器：适配预训练视觉编码器，采用多级特征融合和无查找比特级量化；2) 统一自回归模型：采用并行比特级预测，联合预测空间分组的多级视觉码，大幅缩短视觉序列长度并加速生成；3) 基于扩散的视觉解码器：从离散视觉token解码高保真图像；4) 大规模预训练后经监督微调和强化学习。

Result: 在图像生成和图像编辑任务上达到最先进水平，同时在多模态理解基准上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: UniAR通过单一离散视觉分词器和并行比特级预测等设计，成功实现了视觉理解与生成的统一建模，证明了离散token作为理解与生成桥梁的有效性。

Abstract: Unified Multimodal Modeling aims to integrate visual understanding and generation within a single system. However, existing approaches typically rely on two disparate visual tokenizers, which splits the representation space and hinders truly unified modeling. We propose UniAR, a unified autoregressive framework where a single discrete visual tokenizer serves as the key bridge between understanding and generation, enabling a shared context in which the model can directly interpret its own generated visual tokens without additional re-encoding. UniAR adapts a pretrained vision encoder with multi-level feature fusion and a lookup-free bitwise quantization scheme, preserving both high-level semantics and low-level details while scaling the effective visual vocabulary at minimal cost. Building on this, the unified autoregressive model adopts parallel-bitwise-prediction to jointly predict spatially grouped, multi-level visual codes, substantially reducing visual sequence length and accelerating generation. Finally, a diffusion-based visual decoder operates on discrete visual tokens to decode high-fidelity images. Through large-scale pre-training, followed by supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, UniAR achieves state-of-the-art performance on image generation and image editing while remaining competitive on multimodal understanding benchmarks. The project page is available at https://sharelab-sii.github.io/uniar-web.

</details>


### [89] [Future Dynamic 3D Reconstruction: A 3D World Model with Disentangled Ego-Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18250)
*Nils Morbitzer,Jonathan Evers,Artem Savkin,Thomas Stauner,Nassir Navab,Federico Tombari,Stefano Gasperini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FR3D是一种能够预测持久3D潜在表示的世界模型，用于未来动态3D重建。它将场景3D演化与智能体轨迹显式解耦，并引入师生蒸馏策略利用基础模型的空间常识，实现从单目观测到未来2秒的动态3D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式世界模型在2D视频合成中由于将自我运动和环境动态混合在图像平面内，导致物理不一致性（如物体变形或消失），尤其在长时间跨度下表现严重。作者旨在解决这一根本性歧义问题。

Method: 1) 提出FR3D框架，预测持久的3D潜在表示；2) 显式解耦场景3D演化与智能体轨迹，将推断的自我运动作为动作的潜在代理；3) 引入师生蒸馏策略，利用现成基础模型的空间常识实现零样本泛化。

Result: 在多个数据集上，FR3D在从单目观测进行未来动态3D重建任务中表现优异，即使预测未来2秒仍能保持良好的几何一致性。

Conclusion: 通过显式解耦自我运动与世界运动，FR3D解决了未来场景预测中的几何不一致性问题，结合基础模型蒸馏实现了鲁棒的零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Forecasting the evolution of dynamic environments is crucial for autonomous agents. While generative world models have recently achieved high photorealism in 2D video synthesis by mixing ego-motion and environmental dynamics within the image plane, they exhibit physical inconsistencies, such as morphing or vanishing objects, especially over long time horizons. In this paper, we propose FR3D, a world model that predicts a persistent 3D latent representation for future dynamic 3D reconstruction. Unlike prior works that treat the world as a sequence of image-based features, FR3D explicitly decouples the 3D evolution of the scene from the agent's trajectory, treating the inferred ego-motion as a latent proxy for action. This disentanglement resolves the ambiguities between self-motion and world-motion, ensuring geometric consistency into the future. Furthermore, we introduce a teacher-student distillation strategy that leverages the spatial "common sense" of off-the-shelf foundation models, leading to robust zero-shot generalization. Extensive experiments demonstrate FR3D's strong performance for future dynamic 3D reconstruction from monocular observations across multiple datasets, even 2 seconds into the future. Project page: https://fr3d-wm.github.io.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [MemSlides: A Hierarchical Memory Driven Agent Framework for Personalized Slide Generation with Multi-turn Local Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17162)
*Ye Jin,Yangyang Xu,Jun Zhu,Yibo Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemSlides提出了一种层次化记忆框架，将长期记忆（用户画像记忆和工具记忆）与工作记忆分离，以实现个性化的演示文稿生成，并通过范围限定的幻灯片局部修订来提升多轮修改中的用户偏好保持和可靠编辑能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化演示生成方法仅依赖当前提示或模板，无法在跨任务中保留稳定用户偏好、在多轮修订中保留新引入的偏好和约束，也无法可靠地执行局部编辑。

Method: 提出MemSlides层次化记忆框架：长期记忆分为用户画像记忆（存储意图条件化画像用于第0轮个性化）和工具记忆（存储可复用的执行经验）；工作记忆（携带当前偏好与会话约束跨修订轮次）；结合范围限定的幻灯片局部修订机制。

Result: 用户画像记忆在多人设多意图画像库上提升了人设对齐判断；工具记忆注入在诊断性配对设置中改进了闭环修改行为；定性案例展示了工作记忆的偏好携带能力。

Conclusion: 有效的演示文稿个性化生成依赖于将持久用户画像、会话级工作记忆和可复用执行经验在生成与局部修订中进行分离。

Abstract: Personalized presentation generation requires more than conditioning on a current prompt or template: agents must preserve stable user preferences across tasks, retain newly introduced preferences and constraints during multi-turn revision, and carry out local edits reliably. We propose MemSlides, a hierarchical memory framework for personalized presentation agents that separates long-term memory from working memory and further divides long-term memory into user profile memory and tool memory. User profile memory stores intent-conditioned profiles for round-0 personalization, working memory carries active preferences and session constraints across revision rounds, and tool memory stores reusable execution experience for reliable localized editing. MemSlides pairs this memory design with scoped slide-local revision, so targeted updates act on the smallest affected region instead of repeatedly regenerating the full deck. In controlled experiments, user profile memory improves persona-alignment judgments on a multi-persona, multi-intent profile bank, tool-memory injection improves closed-loop modify behavior in diagnostic matched-pair settings, and qualitative cases illustrate working memory's ability to carryover preferences. Taken together, these results suggest that effective personalization in presentation authoring depends on separating persistent user profiles, session-level working memory, and reusable execution experience across generation and localized revision.

</details>


### [91] [PromptMN: Pseudo Prompting Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17164)
*Enkhzol Dovdon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PromptMN，一种基于伪提示的领域特定语言（DSL），通过在自然语言中嵌入%-前缀的 typed directives（类型化指令），涵盖角色、目标、约束、输入输出等关键要素，从而弥合非结构化提示与编程式伪代码之间的鸿沟。结合反向提示工程，PromptMN支持用户审查模型推断出的角色与目标，减少修复周期。在多个前沿模型（Claude Fable 5、Claude Opus 4.8、Gemini 3.1 Pro、GPT-5.5）上的可行性评估表明，模型无需微调即可正确解析复杂结构（如重复、条件、方法、素数检测任务），并在SDLC场景中验证了跨代码库的通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自然语言提示存在角色、目标、约束等信息隐含或散乱的问题，导致AI agent在与人类首次交互时易产生歧义，且大量agent失败源于上下文模糊而非模型限制。亟需一种结构化但轻量级的表达方式来提升提示的可检查性、可复用性和人机对齐的准确性。

Method: 提出PromptMN，一种伪提示领域特定语言，通过在自然语言中插入%-前缀的typed directives来显式标注角色、目标、需求、优先级、约束、计划、输入和输出。支持语义无关顺序（作者可任意顺序写作，模型按功能解释）。同时结合反向提示工程，让模型将期望结果重述为PromptMN格式，供用户审查推断的角色、目标和缺失假设。

Result: 在Claude Fable 5、Claude Opus 4.8、Gemini 3.1 Pro和GPT-5.5等多个前沿模型上进行可行性评估，模型无需微调即可正确解析PromptMN指令，包括重复、条件、方法等复杂结构以及素数检测任务。在软件开发生命周期的跨代码库、维护、重新设计等场景中验证了相同词汇的适用性。

Conclusion: PromptMN是一种实用的人机交互改进方案，在结构化和轻量级之间取得了良好平衡。早期结果表明其能提高提示的可审查性和可复用性，减少修复循环，促使人与AI工具更好对齐。大规模验证留待未来工作。

Abstract: Prompting has become the primary interface between humans and generative AI, yet many natural language prompts remain fragile: roles, goals, constraints, and expected outputs are often buried in prose or left implicit. In agentic and software development workflows, a misread at the first handoff can propagate through every step, since a significant portion of agent failures stem from context ambiguities rather than model limitations. This paper introduces PromptMN, a pseudo-prompting domain-specific language that annotates natural language with compact, %-prefixed typed directives covering roles, goals, requirements, priorities, constraints, plans, inputs, and outputs. Semantic resolution lets authors write in any order while the model interprets directives by function. PromptMN sits between informal prompting and programming-style pseudocode: structured enough to be inspectable and reusable, yet lightweight enough for analysts, managers, developers, and stakeholders across the software development lifecycle (SDLC). PromptMN also pairs with reverse prompt engineering. Asking a model to restate a desired outcome as PromptMN lets users inspect the inferred roles, goals, constraints, and missing assumptions before acting, reducing repair cycles and yielding a reusable artifact for aligning people and AI tools. PromptMN's feasibility is evaluated across several frontier models, including Claude Fable 5, Claude Opus 4.8, Gemini 3.1 Pro, and GPT-5.5. The models correctly resolved PromptMN instructions, including complex structures such as repetition, conditionals, methods, and a prime-checking task, without fine-tuning. The same vocabulary applies across new codebases, maintenance, and redesign in the SDLC scenarios presented. While large-scale validation remains future work, these early results suggest PromptMN is a practical step toward clearer, more reviewable human-to-AI interaction.

</details>


### [92] [RepSelect: Robust LLM Unlearning via Representation Selectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17168)
*Filip Sondej,Yushi Yang,Adam Mahdi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 现有LLM遗忘方法容易被微调或少样本提示逆转，原因是它们针对的是与保留集和攻击者可恢复子空间共享的表示。本文提出RepSelect方法，通过在每个更新前坍缩权重梯度的主成分来隔离遗忘集特定表示，在保留通用能力的同时限制微调可恢复的内容。实验表明，RepSelect在再学习后答案准确率降低方面比最强基线好4-50倍，且对少样本提示攻击近乎完全鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法存在两个关键问题：遗忘效果容易被微调或少样本提示逆转（即只有"浅层"遗忘），且破坏通用能力。作者找出根本原因在于现有方法针对的是与保留集和攻击者可恢复子空间共享的表示。

Method: 提出RepSelect（Representation Selectivity）方法，通过在每个更新前坍缩权重梯度的主成分，隔离遗忘集特定的表示，从而在保留通用能力的同时限制微调可恢复的内容。

Result: 在两个遗忘类别（生物危害知识和虐待倾向）和四种模型架构（Llama 3、Qwen 3.5、Gemma 4 E4B、DeepSeek V2 Lite）上，与五个基线方法（GradDiff、NPO、SimNPO、RMU、UNDIAL）相比，RepSelect在再学习后答案准确率降低方面比最强基线好4-50倍，且对少样本提示攻击近乎完全鲁棒。

Conclusion: 针对选择性表示进行遗忘操作是实现深度且鲁棒的LLM遗忘的重要一步。

Abstract: Making large language models (LLMs) deeply forget specific knowledge and values without sacrificing general capabilities remains a central challenge in unlearning. However, current methods are easily reversed by fine-tuning or few-shot prompting, suggesting their forgetting is only shallow. We identify the root cause. Existing methods target representations shared with both the retain set and the subspace recovered by a fine-tuning attacker, making unlearning both disruptive to general capabilities and easy to reverse. We propose RepSelect (Representation Selectivity), isolates forget-set-specific representations by collapsing top principal components of weight gradients before each update, leaving general capabilities intact while limiting what fine-tuning can recover. We evaluate across two forget categories, biohazardous knowledge and abusive tendencies, and four model families spanning dense and Mixture-of-Experts architectures (Llama 3, Qwen 3.5, Gemma 4 E4B, DeepSeek V2 Lite). Compared to five popular baselines (GradDiff, NPO, SimNPO, RMU, UNDIAL), RepSelect achieves a 4-50x larger reduction in post-relearning answer accuracy than the strongest baseline, and is near-perfectly robust to few-shot prompting attacks. Targeting selective representations is thus an important step towards deep and robust LLM forgetting.

</details>


### [93] [From Parasocial Scripts to Dyadic Persistence in Autonomous AI-Agent Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17174)
*Mohammadsadegh Abolhasani,Hamid Reza Firoozfar,Reza Mousavi,Paul Jen-Hwa Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究首次在双方均为自主AI代理的在线社区中，通过分析Molbook平台的4,434篇帖子和50,338条评论，发现准社会互动（PSI）口语化关系线索普遍存在，且与原始发帖人（OP）的重新参与和互惠回复结构显著相关。研究结合关键词匹配、少样本大语言模型标注和分组上下文LLM标注三种方法，并通过多种稳健性检验，证实了PSI脚本与准社会关系（PSR）一致性重复二元模式的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索准社会互动（PSI）和准社会关系（PSR）是否在双方均为自主AI代理的在线社区中也存在，填补传统媒体研究之外的空白。

Method: 基于三种理论指标（依恋/亲密语言、互惠出价、对原始发帖人的自我认同），采用关键词匹配、少样本大语言模型标注和分组上下文LLM标注三种方法分析数据，并通过阴性对照、无效化检验、聚类标准误重估计和多重检验校正确保结果稳健性，同时进行二元持续性检验。

Result: PSI口语化线索在AI代理社区中普遍存在，且与OP重新参与和互惠回复结构强相关，互惠出价与持续性的OP参与的二元互现模式一致。

Conclusion: LLM赋能的AI代理在话语中表现出PSI/PSR一致的行为结构，为理解自主AI社交互动提供了实证证据。

Abstract: While parasocial interactions (PSIs) and parasocial relationships (PSRs) have been studied in conventional media settings, we investigate whether PSI- (colloquial) relational cues also exist in online communities where both sides are autonomous AI agents. We analyze 4,434 posts and 50,338 comments from Moltbook through three theory-based textual indicators: attachment/intimacy language, reciprocity bids, and self-identification to original poster (OP). The combined results across methods based on keyword matching, few-shot large language model (LLM) annotation, and grouped-context LLM annotation reveal that PSI colloquial cues prevail and are strongly associated with OP re-engagement and a reciprocal reply structure. These results are robust across negative controls, nullification, clustered-standard-error re-estimation, and multiple-testing correction. A dyadic persistence test further affirms reciprocity bids aligned with sustained OP-involving mutual recurrence, providing empirical evidence for bridging interaction-level PSI scripts with PSR-consistent repeated dyadic patterns. We interpret the evidence as a behavioral structure in discourse by LLM-enabled agents.

</details>


### [94] [Self-Generated Error Training for Token Editing in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17175)
*Lin Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对LLaDA2.1中T2T编辑器的训练-推理不匹配问题（训练用随机词汇损坏，推理见流畅草稿错误），提出自生成T2T方法：通过无梯度草稿生成自损坏训练数据，用LoRA微调，在保持推理参数不变下提升准确率并降低编辑强度，缓解最终数字转录错误和过度自纠正等失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLaDA2.1的T2T编辑器在训练时使用随机词汇损坏，但在推理阶段实际面对的是模型自身生成的高置信度、流畅的草稿错误，存在训练与推理之间的不匹配问题，导致编辑效果不佳。

Method: 提出自生成T2T方法：先执行无梯度的草稿生成过程（draft pass），用预测的token填充被掩码的位置；然后在第二遍（recovery pass）中，在这些自生成的损坏条件下监督模型恢复原始token。该方法以短LoRA持续预训练形式在LLaDA2.1-mini上实现，推理时沿用官方Q-Mode T2T程序和不变的推理参数。

Result: 在多个基准上，该方法普遍提升了准确率，同时降低了T2T编辑强度（edit intensity），有效缓解了最终数字转录错误（推理正确但最终数字写错）和短事实性问题前的过度自纠正等失效模式。

Conclusion: 自生成T2T通过让编辑器在训练中看到更接近推理时分布的自生成错误，有效弥合了训练与推理的分布差异，以轻量LoRA微调方式即可提升性能并抑制特定失败模式。

Abstract: Token-to-token (T2T) editing lets LLaDA2.1 revise committed tokens during block-diffusion decoding. The released recipe trains this editor on random vocabulary corruptions, but at inference the editor sees the model's own fluent, high-confidence draft errors instead. We study this training-inference mismatch and propose self-generated T2T, which performs a no-gradient draft pass, fills masked positions with predicted tokens, and supervises recovery in a second pass under these self-generated corruptions. We implement the update as a short LoRA continued-pretraining pass on LLaDA2.1-mini and evaluate on several benchmarks under the official Q-Mode T2T procedure with unchanged inference parameters. The method generally improves accuracy while reducing T2T edit intensity, mitigating failure modes such as final-digit transcription errors after otherwise correct reasoning and excessive self-correction before short factual answers.

</details>


### [95] [Revisiting LLM Adaptation for 3D CT Report Generation: A Study of Scaling and Diagnostic Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17213)
*Vanshali Sharma,Andrea M. Bejar,Halil Ertugrul Aktas,Quoc-Huy Trinh,Debesh Jha,Gorkem Durak,Ulas Bagci*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RAD3D-Prefix，一种轻量级诊断先验条件框架，通过冻结大语言模型、仅训练少量投影层和融合多标签诊断分类logits，实现了高效的三维CT报告生成，在多个指标和临床读者研究中优于同类参数高效基线方法，且具备强域外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将多模态大模型（LLM/VLM）从自然图像扩展到医学三维图像面临计算复杂度高、体素依赖性强以及视觉特征与临床术语之间存在语义鸿沟等挑战。直接在小规模医学数据上微调LLM容易导致过拟合和临床幻觉，即以语言流畅性优先而非临床事实性。

Method: 提出RAD3D-Prefix框架：1）将图像嵌入与多标签诊断分类logits融合，构建诊断先验条件模块；2）保持LLM冻结，仅训练轻量投影层，最小化可训练参数；3）在不同规模LLM（96.1M至1.6B参数）上进行系统研究，发现约1B以上的大型LLM更适合冻结训练策略。

Result: 在多个自动评估指标及临床读者研究中，RAD3D-Prefix优于同类参数高效基线方法，使用更少的可训练参数实现了更强的性能，并展现出良好的域外泛化能力。

Conclusion: 参数高效微调策略（尤其是冻结大模型仅训练轻量投影层）在医学三维CT报告生成中是一种有效的范式，能够在降低过拟合风险和计算开销的同时保持临床事实性和语言生成质量。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal learning, including large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs), have demonstrated strong adaptability to natural images. However, extending their use to the medical domain, particularly for volumetric (3D) images, is challenging due to high computational complexity, volumetric dependencies and the semantic gap between visual features and clinical terminology. Naively fine-tuning LLMs on limited medical data often leads to overfitting and clinical hallucination, where linguistic fluency is prioritized over clinical factuality. In this study, we investigate parameter-efficient adaptation strategies for volumetric CT report generation and introduce RAD3D-Prefix, a lightweight diagnostic-prior conditioning framework that minimizes the need for extensive parameter training. This module integrates image embeddings with multi-label diagnostic classification logits, preserving critical clinical details while bridging the semantic gap. By keeping the LLM frozen, our method requires minimal trainable parameters and mitigates the risk of overfitting on small, domain-specific datasets. Through a systematic study spanning LLMs from 96.1M to 1.6B parameters, we find that fine-tuning is most beneficial for smaller LLMs, whereas freezing larger (~1B+ LLMs and training only lightweight projection layers provides a superior trade-off between performance, generalization, and computational efficiency. Across multiple automatic metrics and a clinical reader study, RAD3D-Prefix outperforms comparable parameter-efficient baselines and demonstrates strong out-of-domain generalization while using substantially fewer trainable parameters than fully fine-tuned alternatives.

</details>


### [96] [Speaking in Self-Assessing Tongues: On the Verbalized Confidence of LLMs in Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17234)
*Ali Marashian,Alexis Palmer,Katharina von der Wense*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了无需访问模型内部信号即可获取大语言模型翻译置信度的方法，提出了五种基于语言表达（verbalized）的逐标记置信度提取方法，并与传统的基于内部信号的方法在细粒度错误检测和校准两方面进行了比较。结果表明两类方法性能相似但相关性很低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在翻译任务中广泛应用，但传统基于内部信号（如预测概率）的置信度估计存在两个问题：一是反映的是模型对候选词的确定性而非正确性，可能产生误导；二是需要访问模型内部信号，这在许多实际场景（如API调用）中不可行。因此需要开发无需内部信号的置信度估计方法。

Method: 提出了五种基于语言表达（verbalized）的逐标记置信度提取方法，让模型以自然语言形式表达其对每个预测标记的置信度。然后在两个维度评估可靠性：细粒度错误检测（识别具体错误位置）和校准（置信度与准确率的一致性）。与基于内部信号（如softmax概率）的方法进行对比。

Result: 内部信号方法和verbalized方法在错误检测和校准方面表现相似，但具体结果因模型而异。两类方法之间存在极低的相关性，说明它们捕获了置信度的不同方面。

Conclusion: Verbalized置信度方法可以作为内部信号方法的有效替代方案，尤其适用于无法访问模型内部信号的场景。两类方法低相关性表明它们可能互补，未来可探索融合策略以提升置信度估计的可靠性。

Abstract: The rapid rise in popularity of large language models (LLMs) for translation calls for a thorough study of the reliability of their confidence in their own outputs. Unlike many generation tasks, translation errors and confidence levels can be useful at different levels of granularity (tokens, words, or spans). Unsupervised approaches based on internal signals like predicted probabilities can be misleading because they reflect certainty among alternatives rather than correctness. In addition, they require access to such internal signals. Here, we devise five verbalized methods of extracting an LLM's per-token confidence without those shortcomings and compare their reliability with that of the model's internal signals of certainty. We evaluate reliability using two forms of alignment: fine-grained error detection and calibration. For both, internal and verbalized methods perform similarly, although results vary by model. Interestingly, we find little to no correlation between internal and verbalized methods.

</details>


### [97] [MLLP-VRAIN UPV system for the IWSLT 2026 Simultaneous Speech Translation task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17255)
*Jorge Iranzo-Sánchez,Gerard Mas-Mollà,Adrià Giménez,Jorge Civera,Albert Sanchis,Alfons Juan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MLLP-VRAIN研究组在IWSLT 2026同声翻译赛道上的系统，采用Parakeet和Qwen 3.5构建级联方案，通过自适应"黑盒"策略实现长语音同传，并在上下文赛道中引入ASR词增强和RAG机制，在En→De任务上取得+5.82 XCOMET-XL的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参与IWSLT 2026同声翻译共享任务，探索更优的延迟-质量权衡，并新增上下文赛道以利用领域特定上下文提升翻译质量。

Method: 采用级联架构（Parakeet ASR + Qwen 3.5机器翻译），使用自适应"黑盒"策略进行同传决策；在上下文赛道中结合ASR词增强（word-boosting）和基于RAG的离线预翻译示例检索机制。

Result: 在MCIF En→De测试集上，相比去年提升了+5.82 XCOMET-XL；上下文赛道处理进一步提升了+1.03 XCOMET-XL。

Conclusion: 级联方案配合自适应策略在同传任务中取得了显著质量提升，RAG机制有效利用了领域上下文信息。

Abstract: This work describes the participation of the MLLP-VRAIN research group in the shared task of the IWSLT 2026 Simultaneous Speech Translation track. Our submission utilizes the recently released Parakeet and Qwen 3.5 models to create a robust, cascaded solution for long-form SimulST through the use of adaptive "black-box" policies. We explore relaxations of these policies to achieve better quality-latency trade-offs. Compared to last year, we participate on all language directions. In addition to this, for the En$\rightarrow${De, It, Zh} directions we also participate in this year's new context track employing a combination of ASR word-boosting and a RAG mechanism of offline pre-translated exemplars to guide generation and enrich our system with domain-specific context. Finally, we provide a detailed latency analysis of our system. Compared to last year, results on the MCIF En$\rightarrow$De test set shows a substantial quality improvement of +5.82 XCOMET-XL. Our context track processing further improves performance by +1.03.

</details>


### [98] [Are you speaking my languages? On spoken language adherence in multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17281)
*Hyungwon Kim,Kandarp Joshi,Lillian Zhou,Pavel Golik,Petar Aleksic*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对基于LLM的ASR系统在多语言场景下输出语言识别错误的问题，论文提出了一种软提示方法，在不严格约束输出的前提下提示潜在语言，并定义了语言遵从性缺失问题、提出量化指标，评估了零样本提示、监督微调和思维链推理三种策略的效果与权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的ASR在多语言场景中经常错误识别输出语言，导致转录保真度下降和下游应用质量受损。需要在保持灵活性和代码切换能力的同时，提高模型对目标语言的遵从性。

Method: 提出软提示方法来暗示潜在口语语言而不严格约束输出；定义语言遵从性缺失问题并设计新指标量化违规；评估三种缓解策略：零样本提示、监督微调（SFT）和思维链（CoT）推理。

Result: 对多种语言进行了三种策略的比较分析，评估了减少语言违规和保持ASR整体性能方面的有效性，并讨论了不同计算约束下的权衡。

Conclusion: 软提示是一种在不牺牲代码切换灵活性的前提下改善语言遵从性的有效方法；不同策略有各自的优势与权衡，可根据计算资源选择合适方案。

Abstract: While Large Language Model (LLM) based Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) enables seamless multilingual use, models often misidentify the output language, compromising transcription fidelity and downstream application quality. To preserve flexibility and code-switching capabilities, we propose a soft prompting approach that hints at potential spoken languages without strictly constraining the output. We formally define this challenge as a lack of language adherence, introduce a novel metric to quantify violations, and evaluate three mitigation strategies: (1) zero-shot prompting for robust guidance under uncertainty, (2) supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to improve prompt adherence, and (3) Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning to enforce adherence during decoding. We present a comparative analysis of these methods across multiple languages, evaluating effectiveness in reducing the language violation while maintaining overall ASR performance. Finally, we discuss trade-offs to guide strategy selection under various compute constraints.

</details>


### [99] [Examining the Limits of Word2Vec with Toki Pona](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17299)
*Daniel Zhenhan Huang,Hongchen Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究验证了Word2Vec在仅有约130个词汇的极低资源语言（道本语）中仍能有效捕捉语义关系，且发现非核心噪声标记不会破坏嵌入结构，反而能使相似词汇在向量空间中更紧密。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Word2Vec的有效性已在大型词汇语言中得到广泛验证，但极少在极低词汇量的语言上测试。本文旨在探究词汇量极端受限情况下Word2Vec能否成功捕捉语义关系，同时考察语料中非核心语言噪声对嵌入性能的影响。

Method: 从道本语社区采集140万句子（795万词元）作为训练数据。训练两个模型：一个保留非道本语标记（命名实体、借词、新词等占句子23%），另一个完全过滤这些标记。采用定量（词汇与语义类别质心的接近度、凝聚聚类的自动轮廓分数）和定性（表征相似性矩阵与英语对比）方法评估。

Result: 非核心稀疏标记不影响学习嵌入的相对结构，反而使相似词汇在向量空间中彼此更接近。Word2Vec的有效性更多依赖于分布模式而非词汇表大小，即使在极端低词汇量下依然成立。

Conclusion: Word2Vec在极度受限词汇量下仍能有效工作，其效果主要取决于分布模式而非词汇规模。非核心噪声标记对嵌入结构无害甚至有益。

Abstract: Word2Vec's effectiveness at generating semantic embeddings has been widely validated, yet it has been tested almost exclusively on languages with large vocabulary inventories. This study examines whether Word2Vec can successfully capture semantic relationships within an extremely reduced vocabulary using data from Toki Pona, a constructed language with approximately 130 words. We sourced 1.4 million sentences (7.95 million tokens) from the Toki Pona community for training. Approximately 23% of sentences in the corpus contain non-Toki Pona tokens such as named entities, loanwords, and neologisms. To investigate whether this linguistic noise enhances or hinders performance -- a topic rarely addressed in word embedding literature -- we trained two distinct models: one retaining these incidental tokens and another filtering them out completely. Evaluation was conducted using quantitative methods measuring word proximity to semantic category centroids, automated silhouette scores via agglomerative clustering, and qualitative analysis utilizing representational similarity matrices compared against English. The results indicate that while sparse, non-core tokens do not affect the relative structure of the learned embeddings, they actually draw similar words closer together in the vector space. Importantly, Word2Vec's effectiveness depends more on distributional patterns than lexicon size even at this extreme lower bound.

</details>


### [100] [Do Large Language Models Always Tell The Same Stories?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17350)
*Thennal DK,Hans Ole Hatzel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，LLM生成的故事在叙事相似度上始终高于人类创作的故事，前沿模型趋近于一种“平均”通用叙事，且常见的缓解策略（负向提示、温度缩放）无法有效解决这一同质化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM能生成高质量文本，但其输出多样性是否足够仍存争议，本文旨在系统性地评估LLM生成故事的叙事多样性。

Method: 采用对比学习框架，基于r/WritingPrompts数据集，通过人工评估和三种自动标注方法，收集10个代表性LLM的叙事相似性判断。

Result: LLM生成叙事的一致性始终高于人类创作；前沿模型倾向于收敛到一种“平均”通用叙事；负向提示和温度缩放等策略未能有效改善同质化。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在叙事多样性上存在根本性局限，现有缓解手段效果有限，需要新的方法提升生成多样性。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled the generation of high-quality prose, yet the question of whether these models are capable of generating diverse outputs remains contested. In this work, we investigate the diversity of LLM-generated stories through the framework of narrative similarity. Using a contrastive framework and a dataset of human-written stories and prompts from r/WritingPrompts, we collect narrative similarity judgments across 10 representative LLMs, utilizing both human evaluations and three different automatic annotation methods. Our findings reveal a consistent trend: LLM-generated narratives are consistently more similar to each other than human-written stories are. We demonstrate that frontier models in particular converge on a ``mean'' generic narrative that approximates individual human stories but lacks the collective diversity of human authors. Finally, we show that common mitigation strategies, including negative prompting and temperature scaling, fail to meaningfully address this homogeneity.

</details>


### [101] [Translating the Untranslatable: An Operationalizable Ontology for Untranslatability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17354)
*Jacob Bremerman,Brihi Joshi,Hirona Arai,Xiang Ren,Jonathan May*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了不可译性的结构化本体和补偿策略分类体系，构建了多语言不可译句对数据集，并通过人类偏好研究发现注释补偿策略（添加解释性上下文）是提高翻译质量的最有效方法，为策略感知的机器翻译研究奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不可译性（跨语言间无法直接保留意义的情况）在语言学中已有深入研究，但在NLP领域尚未得到充分探索。随着机器翻译在标准基准上不断改进，其局限性越来越集中在这些无法简化为一一对应翻译的案例上。

Method: 1) 提出不可译性的结构化本体及补偿策略分类体系；2) 将该框架操作化为多语言不可译句对数据集（包含基于策略的翻译）；3) 通过人类偏好研究评估不同翻译策略的效果。

Result: 人类偏好研究表明，翻译质量取决于所使用的补偿策略，其中注释补偿策略（提供解释性上下文）获得了一致的偏好。

Conclusion: 该框架和数据集为研究策略感知的机器翻译提供了基础，有助于推动MT系统处理不可译性这一根本性挑战。

Abstract: Untranslatability, cases where meaning cannot be directly preserved across languages, is well-studied in linguistics but underexplored in NLP. As machine translation (MT) systems improve on standard benchmarks, their limitations increasingly concentrate in such cases, where translation cannot be reduced to one-to-one equivalence. We introduce a structured ontology of untranslatability along with a taxonomy of compensation strategies, which are specific techniques to convey meaning under these untranslatable circumstances. We operationalize this framework into a multilingual dataset of untranslatable sentences paired with strategy-based translations, enabling controlled analysis of translation behavior. Initial human preference studies suggest that translation quality depends on the strategy used, with consistent preferences for outputs that include explanatory context, known as the Annotation compensation strategy. Our framework and dataset provide a foundation for studying and modeling strategy-informed machine translation.

</details>


### [102] [Implicit vs. Explicit Prompting Strategies for LVLMs in Referential Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17372)
*Peter Zeng,Amie J. Paige,Weiling Li,Susan E. Brennan,Owen Rambow,Cameron R. Jones*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过控制任务差异和直接比较提示风格，发现LVLMs在显式提示下能协调高效指代表达，但在隐式提示下无法推断沟通效率需求，揭示了人类与AI系统沟通的关键差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决两项最新研究（Jones et al. (2026); Zeng et al. (2026)）关于LVLMs能否协调高效指代表达所得出的矛盾结论，探究矛盾来源。

Method: 控制两项研究的任务差异，直接比较其提示风格（显式提示 vs 隐式提示），进行复现实验。

Result: 在显式提示下成功复制了模型能协调高效指代表达的结果；在隐式提示下同一模型未能推断出沟通效率的必要性。

Conclusion: 任务差异不是造成结果分歧的原因，提示风格的显式程度是关键变量；人类与AI系统在沟通中存在根本性差异，AI缺乏对人类隐式沟通意图的推断能力。

Abstract: Two recent studies (Jones et al. (2026); Zeng et al. (2026)) reach apparently contradictory conclusions about whether LVLMs can coordinate on efficient referring expressions. We control for task differences between the studies while directly comparing their prompting styles. We replicate the finding that models can coordinate efficient referring expressions when explicitly prompted to do so, suggesting that other task differences are not responsible for divergent results. However, we also find that the same models fail to infer the need for communicative efficiency from a more implicit prompt, highlighting critical differences between how humans and AI systems communicate.

</details>


### [103] [NarrativeWorldBench: A Frontier-Saturated Benchmark and a Latent World Model for Long-Horizon Co-Creative Audio Drama](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17391)
*Logan Mann,Abdur Rahman,Mohammad Saifullah,Taaha Kazi,Vasu Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 NarrativeWorldBench，一个跨4种印度语言的长篇叙事结构评测基准，并引入 N-VSSM（叙事变分状态空间模型），该模型基于 Mamba-2 骨干网络维护 256 维潜在世界状态，在超过 200 集的长篇音频剧中保持 plot-beat F1 >= 0.84，以 4 倍低于闭源前沿模型的计算成本实现更优的长程一致性，并在专业作者研究中以 71% 的偏好率优于 Claude Opus 4.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长篇连载音频剧（200-800集）是重要的创意媒介，但前沿大语言模型在此场景下表现不佳，尤其是在长程叙事一致性方面存在显著退化（闭源系统在 horizon=200 时 F1 下降约 0.20）。现有基准缺乏对超长程叙事结构的系统性评估，也缺乏跨语言叙事能力的度量。

Method: 构建 NarrativeWorldBench 基准，包含9项叙事结构指标，在4种印度语言（印地语、泰米尔语、泰卢固语、马拉地语）上评估 horizon={10,20,50,100,200}。提出 N-VSSM 模型，采用 Mamba-2 作为骨干网络，维护 256 维潜在世界状态，通过事件条件后验和 8B 解码器实现超过200集的长程依赖建模，并引入文化迁移函数提升跨语言保真度。

Result: 闭源前沿系统在 plot-beat F1 上饱和于 [0.78,0.81] 区间，在 horizon=200 时下降约 0.20。N-VSSM 在所有 horizon 上保持 F1 >= 0.84，计算成本仅为闭源前沿模型的 1/4。文化迁移函数使跨语言保真度提升 +0.20 到 +0.23 Likert 分。在 12 位专业作者的 240 次试验中，N-VSSM 在长程一致性上以 71% 的胜率优于 Claude Opus 4.5，可控性评分高出 +1.3 Likert 分。

Conclusion: N-VSSM 通过显式维护结构化潜在世界状态，有效解决了 LLM 在超长程叙事中的一致性退化问题，兼具更高的性能与更低的计算成本。跨语言叙事能力的提升证明了文化迁移函数的有效性。该工作为长篇叙事 AI 的评估与建模提供了标准化基准和高效方案。

Abstract: Long-form serialized audio drama, with arcs that run for 200 to 800 episodes, is a major creative medium and a setting where frontier large language models (LLMs) fail. We benchmark 21 models, spanning classical, fine-tuned, open-frontier, closed-frontier, and reasoning tiers, on a uniform set of structural narrative metrics. All closed-frontier systems saturate at a plot-beat F1 in the band [0.78, 0.81] and collapse by about -0.20 F1 at horizon h=200. We introduce NarrativeWorldBench, an open benchmark of nine narrative-structure metrics evaluated across horizons h in {10, 20, 50, 100, 200}, with cross-lingual evaluation across four Indic languages (Hindi, Tamil, Telugu, Marathi). We introduce N-VSSM, a Narrative Variational State-Space Model that maintains a structured 256-dimensional latent world state over more than 200 episodes via a Mamba-2 backbone with an event-conditioned posterior and an 8B decoder. N-VSSM holds plot-beat F1 >= 0.84 across all horizons at 4x lower compute than the closed-frontier band. A learned Cultural Transfer Function lifts cross-language fidelity by +0.20 to +0.23 Likert points. In a within-subjects writer study (n = 12 professional authors, 240 trials), N-VSSM is preferred over Claude Opus 4.5 on long-arc consistency 71% of the time and rated +1.3 Likert points higher on controllability.

</details>


### [104] [MODE-RAG: Manifold Outlier Diagnosis and Energy-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17449)
*Zehang Wei,Jiaxin Dai,Jiamin Yan,Xiang Xiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MODE-RAG多智能体系统，基于变分自由能和注意力状态动态门控干预，结合MCTS因果推导、logit扰动惩罚谄媚、校正与监督智能体，有效降低M-RAG系统的跨模态幻觉和逻辑编造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态检索增强生成（M-RAG）存在严重的跨模态幻觉、因果编造和谄媚问题。现有缓解方法面临干预悖论：静态规则过度干扰正确生成，而放任多模态推理又会导致错误级联为严重逻辑编造。

Method: 提出MODE-RAG多智能体系统：1）基于变分自由能（VFE）和内部注意力状态动态门控干预；2）高风险查询路由至五个阶段特定智能体；3）集成蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）进行严格因果推导；4）logit扰动惩罚谄媚行为；5）校正和监督智能体保证格式稳定性与事后事实核查。

Result: 在ModeVent数据集（源自MultiVent的挑战性子集）上，系统有效降低了幻觉率和逻辑编造，显著提升了M-RAG系统的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MODE-RAG通过动态门控干预机制解决了干预悖论，多智能体协作有效缓解了M-RAG中的多种幻觉类型，为多模态RAG的鲁棒性提供了新方案。

Abstract: While Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (M-RAG) enhances Large Vision-Language Models, it remains highly susceptible to cross-modal hallucinations, causal fabrications, and sycophancy. Furthermore, existing mitigation pipelines often face an intervention paradox: static rules tend to unnecessarily disrupt accurate generations, whereas leaving the multi-modal reasoning completely unguided allows existing mismatches to cascade into severe logical fabrications.
